THE FIGURES 2,257,401, 112,750 AND 40,015 THAT STOOD HERE BEFORE THE REPAIR PASS WERE RIGHT FOR THE FILES AS THEY THEN STOOD AND ARE SUPERSEDED BY THIS LINE.**

**AND WHAT A SUCCESSOR TAKES FROM IT, IN ONE PARAGRAPH.** A practice on a weigh-house floor four days off that says a number out loud twice at the fourth hour on the five mornings a week a room upstairs is shut, which said eight on every morning it ran until nine days of water took the shoulder and says four on every morning it has run since, out of a calendar count of one hundred and fifty-four mornings of which six were lost and a run count of one hundred and forty-eight, and a figure in one woman's hand on the back leaf of a day-book that is not the record and that no clerk will look at; nine hurdles across a cart track at the head of a made road and nine houses about four hundred yards back with a light in one window of the fourth of them at about the ninth hour and no name against the road and the hurdles set by the people at the end of it who would like them to stay; twenty-nine words said through a shut door and a name given and nothing asked for in return; a man at a trough who counts what does not come up and who told a stranger to write a thing down and not to say where; a board with nine blanks on it that stayed blank; a claim at the boards that a bar will get up that shut track, made by a roadkeeper who said in the same sentence that he will not try it; a practice with two holes in it that nobody has written down in any room; nine people who said no on the first of Mudmonth and who have not since offered anybody else a place to put their name; a wall with nine feet of gap and four hundred and seventy-nine days of stone carried and the ninth yard not started; a printed notice a hundred and twenty days past its date that has shut nothing; and the use of one ear and the use of one leg and the standing fact that being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour.

### Review repair pass on Batch 0003, from `logs/batch-0003.review.log`, what was applied and what was not

**Fourteen findings, fourteen applied, in the order the review gave them, and two further defects of the same class were found and applied without being asked for.** Nothing was restarted and no plot beat was moved.

**One, the drove dates.** Nine of the ten drove paragraphs date the Thursday before their own dateline and eight of those nine were wrong. Mudmonth runs 2706 to 2765 at sixty days and every other month is thirty, so the sixty-fifth, the seventy-second and the ninety-first of Mudmonth could not exist, and the other five were dates belonging to the wrong week. Corrected to Mudmonth 49, Mudmonth 49, Mudmonth 56, Longlight 3, Longlight 10, Longlight 15, Longlight 22, Rainmonth 1, Rainmonth 12 and Rainmonth 27. **The drove counts 59 / 59 / 60 / 61 / 62 / 63 / 64 / 65 / 67 / 69 were already right and were not touched, because the counts come from the day number and the dates came from somewhere else, and the finding is that those are two different tasks that this manuscript had been doing as one.**

**Two, `chapter-0772.md`'s spine.** The dateline printed a walk of about nine hours over a body that walks the eleven miles between the fifth hour and the ninth, so the dateline now prints the two hours and notes that four hours is under the six a sound man takes. The roadkeeper was placed a mile short of the weigh-house, which is south of the man he is speaking to and on the wrong side of the boundary his own speech turns on, so he is now a mile short of the trough at the bottom of the cart track, four miles above his own four miles, and the four miles he keeps are named as the four miles below the weigh-house, which the man of thirty-two walked on his way up and had not thought of as anybody's. The soft place the protagonist names is that same place below the weigh-house and he is asked about it from a mile and a half away because he saw it on his way up, and the straw is now given to somebody else, because `chapter-0769.md` lays it and it is the woman of about fifty-one who lays it. And the ninth hour of that Wednesday can no longer be printed eleven miles apart, so he turns back at the gate, sleeps the night on the dry side of the trough and walks down on the Thursday morning.

**Three, the chalk.** `chapter-0770.md` put four short lines on a stone sill **where the rain does not reach** and said outright that lines on stone are not stopped by water. `chapter-0771.md` then ran nine days of rain down them and `chapter-0778.md` called them soft in the rain in the same breath as calling the sill sheltered, which is the block's founding image contradicting itself. The four lines are now dry and sharp under the hood with the rain in the yard around them, and what he misreads is a pale thumb-drag across the end of the top line, which is the man of about fifty-four's own chalk from the Thursday before. The cost codas at `chapter-0771.md` and `chapter-0780.md` now print four lines under a sill the rain does not reach.

**Four, the System panel** is seventeen words and not the fifteen that three state files carried, and it is now counted on the panel as printed with the bold markers off.

**Five, the back leaf of the day-book** carried nine figures on one page and a figure on another; it is nine figures and a date on every page that prints it.

**Six, `chapter-0777.md`** reported at its fifteenth line that four hundred people in that market street had already been told the thing that morning, which the chapter does not reach until its thirty-fifth line. It now says they had no idea it was there.

**Seven, `chapter-0771.md`'s dateline** called him a man of thirty-four with a stick, and he is thirty-two, and a man of about thirty-four with a satchel is a separate figure who says the number on that floor at `chapter-0776.md`.

**Eight, `chapter-0780.md`** printed a garbled nine feet off at the wall, which is now nine feet off her at the wall and keeps the fourth of this county's four nine-foot things.

**Nine, the promise.** He says out loud in a wet yard at `chapter-0771.md` that he will go up on the Monday, and then goes on the Wednesday and the Monday is never mentioned again. The yard now says the Wednesday and `chapter-0772.md` carries it.

**Ten, `state/phase-ledger.json`** is controller-owned and was not touched, and a phase that reports that finding without touching the file is behaving correctly.

**Eleven, `workspace/volume-16/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` did not exist** and now does. `AGENTS.md` requires the finishing phase to write exactly one next-batch prompt, and the workflow falls back to a one-paragraph generic continuation prompt when no volume prompt exists, which is not this project's practice.

**Twelve, the block prompt's own five-and-five on market mornings is wrong** and the ten datelines give two, and `state/current.md` and `state/chapter-summaries.md` had carried the wrong count out of the prompt.

**Thirteen, Basis B 299 was under-specified** and all five of its exclusions are now named, being ten datelines, twenty-three dialogue paragraphs, one System panel, ten cost codas and ten money columns.

**Fourteen, one sentence frame carried two hundred and thirty-five sentences** and a hundred and eighteen of them were re-voiced across the ten files, so the batch now carries have been saying that forty times, have said to each other that thirty-nine, have kept saying that thirty-nine and have said since that one hundred and seventeen, **and the ten chapters no longer answer the printed request of the fifteenth of Frostmonth in the same eleven words, which they had all been doing.**

**The two applied without being asked for.** `chapter-0775.md` had him walking down eleven miles from a cart-track head he had promised at `chapter-0774.md` not to go up a third time, and reaching a trough an hour before the market town three hours after it. He now goes up to the trough on the Saturday before and lies two nights under the hedge beside it and comes down on the Monday, which keeps the chapter, the trough, the man at the trough and his twenty-six words exactly as they were. And `chapter-0774.md` printed nine days between two trips that are six days apart, in the dateline, in the chorus and in the sentence about what he got wrong.

**What did not change and what a successor may not undo.** The reversal is still unpaid in the sense that nothing was fixed: the nine hurdles are still across the track, still set by the people at the end of it, and no route was opened, shut or cut, no hurdle was lifted, nothing was cut and the mechanism is still unsaid. No name was spent or unspent and two of the volume's six remain for Blocks 0004 and 0005, one in each. No System panel was added. Nobody was thanked. The manuscript is 2,257,801 words in seven hundred and eighty chapters.

## Volume 16, Batch 0004 — Chapters 781 to 790, "The Roll", days 2827 to 2898

**What the block is, in one sentence.** A roll of nine lines comes up a made road in the ordinary post, is nailed to a board in a market street, is read by about four hundred people, is answered out loud three times in five days by three people who do not agree with each other and of whom none of them is the protagonist, is not filled in and is not torn up, comes back a second time with a sentence on a folded sheet in a hand nobody claimed, carries the name of the man who sent it at the foot of a covering paper for the first time in the volume, and has a Tuesday printed at the head of it that comes and shuts nothing.

**The three answers, and why they cannot be held together.** A woman of about forty-six who keeps a lime kiln says she is not going on a line under somebody else's hand. A man of about twenty-seven who has driven about two hundred and thirty loads of lime out of Wethergill unpaid says a road with no name against it is a road nobody can shut. A woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a room and is paid about fourpence a year says a form nobody is paid to fill is a form a county cannot fill. **The first refuses every name, the second insists a name is the keeper's own and is not to be surrendered, and the third insists a county which will not fill a form is a county which will not govern. No county of about two thousand people can hold two of them and none of them is wrong and that is the difficulty.** The protagonist says nothing at all, at `chapter-0782.md`, at `chapter-0783.md`, at `chapter-0784.md` where nine people ask him four questions and he answers none, and at `chapter-0790.md`.

**The one name it spent, and the one printing it spent.** `Brida Lenth` is printed once, at `chapter-0782.md`, and is the fifth of the volume's six. `Pell Arkwright` is printed once and is a return. **The antagonist's name is printed once, at `chapter-0781.md`, in a document, at the foot of a covering paper, under the Marshal's hand, and never in a mouth, and that is the first of the volume's two and the second and last is Batch 0005's.** One slot remains for Batch 0005 and it is the last of the six.

**The date it moved and why.** One dateline and one only, being 2843 moved to 2835, so that the three answers could be given on Thursday 2831, Saturday 2833 and Monday 2835, inside one week, which is what the block's own instruction asks for. The other nine datelines are as the prompt gave them.

**The refusal is paid and is not resolved.** The roll printed the twenty-third of Fallowmonth, a Tuesday. Nothing was shut. The page says the refusal was paid on that Tuesday and that being paid is not the same as being finished, and the roll is still on the board with nine empty lines on it and a second copy nailed over the first, and nobody in nine counties has written to anybody about why the fourth line is empty.

**The hand-off, in three parts, none of it conducted.** A refusal in writing that is also a refusal to govern stands in forty-five words in a lane at `chapter-0783.md` and in one sentence on a folded sheet inside the covering paper of the second copy at `chapter-0788.md`, in a hand that is not the hand of any clerk this county has ever had, and nobody has said who wrote it and nobody is going to be asked. A sealed route that a roadkeeper has claimed can be made to speak stands with nothing tried at it, and at `chapter-0784.md` he is at the boards with a bar in his hand and says nothing at all. An occupation with a gate on it and a keeper who is a local is set up in this block's own terms at `chapter-0790.md` by the badge-man of about fifty-eight writing nothing on a roll and by a counter and a door at the low end of that market street with a man behind it who was born in that street, and it is not entered.

**What the block refused to do, and it is the whole list.** No System panel. No second printing of the name in a mouth or outside a document. No route opened, closed or cut, no hurdle lifted, no bar tried at that shut track, no third trip up the cart track, no stone laid, no mallet picked up, no head filled or ruled, no finding under any of the ten questions, no half leaf turned, no cloth lifted, no ninth yard started, no scar cured or called small, no ninth stone looked over and no far side shown, no road filled in or shut or torn up, no name put against a road, no refusal of Volume 15 reopened and neither of its two men asked anything, no second question to the man of forty-one and no scene with him in it, no account asked of the woman of about fifty-one about the broom straw, no account asked of any of the nine who refused the roll on the first of Mudmonth, no body made of the nine houses or the nine households or the nine who refused, no practice put in the register or the day-book or under a fifth head, no declaration about the two of them and nothing about them in any document, no mechanism explained, no roadmark named, no separate leaf looked at, no palm noticed, nobody sent to the city, no shut road reopened, no quiet road traced, no carter's mate found, no fifth hole opened, no band figure printed, and **nobody thanked in any chapter and in any state file this block wrote.**

**The measurements, recounted on the files and not inherited, and recounted again after the review repair pass at `state/continuity.md` 1759 onward.** Per file **4,122 / 4,380 / 4,172 / 3,772 / 3,812 / 3,689 / 4,034 / 4,054 / 4,209 / 4,273**, block total **40,517**, mean **4,051.7 and 4,051 truncated**, minimum 3,689 at `chapter-0786.md` and maximum 4,380 at `chapter-0782.md`, no file below three thousand words. `about` **22.80 / 23.29 / 24.93 / 24.92 / 26.76 / 26.84 / 26.77 / 23.68 / 26.37 / 24.10** per 1,000, floor at `chapter-0781.md` and ceiling at `chapter-0786.md`, **no page at or above 27.00 on a case-sensitive count of the lowercase token `about`, and on a case-insensitive count the ceiling is 27.02 at `chapter-0787.md`, which is why the instrument is named with the band.** The figures before the repair pass were **4,118 / 4,289 / 4,190 / 3,790 / 3,830 / 3,707 / 4,052 / 4,072 / 4,227 / 4,288**, a total of **40,563** and a mean of **4,056.3**, and are superseded. **The census on the instrument named at `state/continuity.md` 1708 is Basis A 304 paragraphs and 0 openers at 0.0 per cent and Basis B 270 and 0 at 0.0 per cent, and this block prints no System panel and therefore Basis B has FOUR named exclusions and not five, being the ten datelines, the four dialogue paragraphs, the ten cost codas and the ten closing money columns, and 304 less ten less four less ten less ten is 270.** The thank family is thirty uses and every one of the thirty is a negation. **The duplicate-paragraph count over all seven hundred and ninety files is thirty-six groups and thirty-seven surplus occurrences and THIS BLOCK ADDS ZERO OF BOTH**, and the same instrument over the prior seven hundred and eighty files gives the same thirty-six and thirty-seven.

**The money, with the collision set rebuilt rather than inherited, and rebuilt again with the instrument written out.** The instrument is the printed total in every spelling across the whole hundred band, **and every spelling means all four of the hundreds this manuscript uses, being *one hundred*, *two hundred*, *three hundred* and *a hundred*, each with the remainder as *and* the tens or as the tens with no *and*, plus the bare digit with a non-digit on either side of it,** run over every file whole. **On the instrument as it was worded here it gave 284 printed values in the band sixty to three hundred and forty-three and ZERO free ones, and THAT RESULT IS WITHDRAWN, being an artifact of pairing every remainder with every hundred so that *a hundred and sixty-six* was counted as a printing of two hundred and sixty-six. On the corrected instrument at `state/continuity.md` 1761 the same run gives 279 printed and five free, being 218, 266, 273, 326 and 329, and the usable free set is still zero because all five fail the barred-over-figure filter at 26, 26, 33, 38 and 41, and the 281 and three this summary used to carry is withdrawn and is not reproducible, the correction being at `state/continuity.md` 1821, and nothing under sixty is free either, so the route below sixty is shut on the arithmetic alone. The withdrawal is recorded at `state/open-threads.md` 749. THE FIGURE THIS PARAGRAPH PRINTED BEFORE THE REPAIR PASS, being 272 printed and twelve free, is withdrawn: five of the twelve are demonstrably printed and the instrument behind them omitted the *a hundred and …* spelling. The full instrument and the failure are at `state/continuity.md` 1761.** This block's ten totals are 91, 104, 127, 147, 176, 199, 221, 247, 283 and 306 pence, ten distinct and ascending, all ten printed already, and **all ten closing paragraphs name the reuse on the page and give the reason, which is arithmetic, and say the band has been searched whole again, and use *searched whole*, *barred numeral*, *route below sixty* and *file in the manuscript* exactly once each and not one word more.** Every column foots four ways with the forty-eight-pence conversion checked separately from the sum, and the over-figures are 43, 8, 31, 3, 32, 7, 29, 7, 43 and 18, and no barred numeral is a price or an over-figure or a printed total in any of the ten.

**All four printed speeches foot off the words printed beside them**, being thirty-one and forty-two at `chapter-0782.md` and sixteen and forty-five at `chapter-0783.md`, and no unprinted speech carries a count. **And the banned words are at zero across the ten files, being stage, ledger, qualification, failed, charter, custodian, mechanism, small, volume and block, and the barred kin-terms are at zero across them, being all six of the set named at `state/continuity.md` 1647 and not two of it, being mother, father, son, daughter, brother and sister. This summary's earlier count of twelve named ten and then sister and brother, and called those two the barred kin-terms, and that re-cut is withdrawn at `state/continuity.md` 1820.**

**What the block changed in the shape of the volume.** The cull's machinery is now a piece of paper with a date on it and nine lines on it, and it is not the Wound and it is not a person and it is a roll. **The volume's antagonist has been named once, in a document, and the reader now has a name to attach to a piece of paper that has already cost about two hundred and thirty loads of lime and nine hundred feet of unpaid kerb and a Tuesday that shut nothing.** The volume's ninth chapter-shape question, *who is going to hold nine hundred yards of wall*, has been answered for the purposes of this block by a different question: who is going to write a name on a line, and the answer is three incompatible noes and a silence.

**What the block did not change and what a successor may not undo.** A closed path is not destroyed and its silence is a political loss and no power available to anybody in this volume repairs it. Four lines on a sill are not the beginning of a cure and a figure in a woman's hand on the back of a book is not a cure either and a sentence in an unclaimed hand on a folded sheet is not a cure either. Being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour and nobody was told so and nobody wrote it down. The nine hurdles are still across the head of that cart track, put there by the people at the end of it who would like them to stay. The ninth yard of the wall has not been started. The mallet has not been picked up. The half leaf is face down. The cloth is over the bench. Nobody has been up that track a third time. Nothing was opened and nothing was cut. **And the planned ending is untouched and no final enemy was introduced.**

## Volume 16, Batch 0005 — Chapters 791 to 800, "The Lower Anchor", days 2899 to 2970

**This is the FIFTH AND LAST BLOCK OF VOLUME 16 AND IT IS THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX AND ITS RESOLUTION IN THE SAME SEVENTY-TWO DAYS, and the block runs to Chapter 800 and there is no Batch 0006 and no Volume 17 prompt, and the only successor this block owes is the volume close record `outline/volumes/volume-16-close.md`, whose prompt is `workspace/volume-16/close/PROMPT.md` and which is on disk.** The block runs seventy-two days and carries **two** month boundaries, being Wolfmonth the first at day 2916, a Friday and not a market morning, and Frostmonth the first at day 2946, a Sunday and not a market morning, **and no chapter is dated on either of them.** **THE TEN DATELINES ARE 2899, 2908, 2915, 2924, 2932, 2940, 2948, 2957, 2963 AND 2970, being Embermonth the fourteenth, the twenty-third and the thirtieth, Wolfmonth the ninth, the seventeenth and the twenty-fifth, and Frostmonth the third, the twelfth, the eighteenth and the twenty-fifth, all YR 323, with the weekdays Tuesday, Thursday, Thursday, Saturday, Sunday, Monday, Tuesday, Thursday, Wednesday and Wednesday, and FOUR OF THE TEN ARE MARKET MORNINGS AND SIX ARE NOT, the four being 2908, 2915, 2924 and 2957.** The block moved no dateline. Every weekday was derived from the day number and the single anchor at day 2310 and never copied out of either printed table, **and the table at `outline/volume-16.md` section seventeen is wrong in four rows, two of them beside this block, being 2916 which it prints as a Sunday and which is a Friday and 2946 which it prints as a Thursday and which is a Sunday.** The market mornings inside the seventy-two days are twenty and fifty-two mornings carry the room shut.

**What the block did, in one sentence.** Nine people held nine things by saying a number out loud twice at the fourth hour for eleven mornings without the man of thirty-two in it, a gate at the top of about nine hundred yards of a stair fell to people with a Crown's warrant on a morning he was four hours off standing at the ninth of nine stones and did not know for twelve days, a third copy of the roll arrived proposing a man by his trade and signed at the foot under a name nobody read out loud, a woman of about fifty-one who keeps an inn was named, and a man of thirty-two said forty-two words at a trestle table in front of about four hundred people about himself and about nine of them stopped.

**The eleven mornings, which are a count of mornings inside seventy-two days and not a count of chapters.** He was on that floor on the first of Wolfmonth and was not on it on any of the eleven mornings after it up to and including the seventeenth, being the third, the fourth, the fifth, the sixth, the eighth, the tenth, the eleventh, the twelfth, the thirteenth, the fifteenth and the seventeenth, and every one of those eleven is a morning the room is shut and the practice runs. On all eleven the number was said twice by two people who were not him and eight of the ten things were held on all eleven and nothing about any of it was written down anywhere.

**The two costs this block puts on the page for the first time in the volume, both plain and both unexplained.** The bleed, which starts on the fifth of Wolfmonth at about the fourth hour at the ninth of the nine stones and is warm and does not stop when it is pressed and has not faded and is fifty days old at the last page, and which nobody in nine counties knows about, which is in no document, which has never been said out loud in a room, and for which no panel and no panel-shaped paragraph is printed. And the last clear thing he will ever have of his mother, which is one sentence she said to him before he came into this county, heard clearly at the fourth hour of the twenty-fifth of Wolfmonth and not after that, said by nobody, written down nowhere, and the ear it was heard on is the side the stick is on and is permanent.

**The name.** `Bessie Dellow`, about fifty-one, who keeps the inn at the high end of that market street and keeps a tally-stick, first printed at `chapter-0792.md`, given the way everything in this county gives a name, being that a carter asks her one question kindly in his own trade and she answers. **It is the sixth and last name of Volume 16's budget of six and the budget is now closed, and `Pell Arkwright` printed again is a return and costs nothing.**

**The antagonist's name, twice in the volume and never again.** The first printing is at `chapter-0781.md` on the covering paper of the first copy of the roll. The second and last is at `chapter-0797.md` inside the covering paper of the third copy, on an endorsement sheet in a clerk's hand that proposes the man who keeps the weigh-house floor as the keeper of that county's roads by his trade, with the Marshal's hand written out in full at the foot of it. **Both are documents. Neither is in a mouth. Neither was read out loud in any room. The cap of two is now spent and no chapter of any later volume may print it again.**

**The hand-off, in three parts, none of it conducted.** A refusal in writing that is also a refusal to govern stands in forty-five words in a lane at `chapter-0783.md` and in one sentence on a folded sheet inside the covering paper of the second copy at `chapter-0788.md`, and no second letter went out of this county in this block and nobody was asked who wrote the first. A sealed route that a roadkeeper has claimed can be made to speak is carried forward at `chapter-0795.md` with nothing tried at it and the roadkeeper not in that street and not up that made road since the twenty-fourth of Fallowmonth. An occupation with a gate on it and a keeper who is a local is set up at `chapter-0798.md` by a badge-man of about fifty-eight writing nothing at all on a roll and by a counter and a door at the low end of that market street with a man behind it who was born in that street, and it is not entered.

**What the block refused to do, and it is the whole list.** No System panel. No third printing of the antagonist's name and none in a mouth. No route opened, closed or cut, no hurdle lifted, no bar tried at that shut track, no third trip up the cart track, no stone laid, no mallet picked up, no head filled or ruled, no finding under any of the ten questions, no half leaf turned, no cloth lifted, no ninth yard started, no scar cured or called small or said to be closing over, no ninth stone looked over and no far side shown, no road filled in or shut or torn up, no name put against a road, no refusal of Volume 15 reopened and neither of its two men asked anything, no second question to the man of forty-one and no scene with him in it, no account asked of the woman of about fifty-one about the broom straw, no account asked of any of the nine who refused the roll on the first of Mudmonth, no body made of the nine houses or the nine households or the nine who refused, no practice put in the register or the day-book or under a fifth head, no declaration about the two of them and nothing about them in any document and no chapter ending on them, no mechanism explained, no roadmark named, no separate leaf looked at, no palm noticed, no Ilyra Kest route notes produced or mentioned, nobody sent to the city, no shut road reopened, no quiet road traced, no carter's mate found, no path count converted into main-world days and no path mouth monthed, no fifth hole opened, no band figure printed, no new final enemy, and **nobody thanked in any chapter and in any state file this block wrote.**

**The measurements, recounted on the files and not inherited.** Per file **4,408 / 4,128 / 4,164 / 4,185 / 4,425 / 4,251 / 4,441 / 4,290 / 4,260 / 4,396**, block total **42,948**, mean **4,294.8 and 4,294 truncated**, minimum 4,128 at `chapter-0792.md` and maximum 4,441 at `chapter-0797.md`, no file below three thousand words. **THE PRE-REPAIR COUNTS OF 4,359 / 4,090 / 4,123 / 4,146 / 4,374 / 4,222 / 4,414 / 4,257 / 4,234 / 4,359, OF 42,578 AND OF THE `about` COLUMN THAT FOLLOWED THEM ARE WITHDRAWN BY THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS RECORDED AT `state/continuity.md` 1779 TO 1782.** `about` **22.91 / 24.22 / 22.33 / 25.33 / 24.63 / 23.05 / 21.39 / 25.64 / 24.65 / 21.84** per 1,000, floor at `chapter-0797.md` and ceiling at `chapter-0798.md`, **and no page is at or above 27.00, and the figure is a case-insensitive count of the token `about`, which is the safer of the two counts to write to.** **The census on the instrument named at `state/continuity.md` 1708 is Basis A 324 paragraphs and 0 openers at 0.0 per cent and Basis B 291 and 0 at 0.0 per cent, and this block prints no System panel and therefore Basis B has FOUR named exclusions and not five, being the ten datelines, the three dialogue paragraphs, the ten cost codas and the ten closing money columns, and 324 less ten less three less ten less ten is 291.** The opener count is zero for the third block running. The thank family is thirty-three uses and every one of the thirty-three is a negation. **The duplicate-paragraph count over all eight hundred files is thirty-six groups and thirty-seven surplus occurrences and THIS BLOCK ADDS ZERO OF BOTH**, and the same instrument over the seven hundred and ninety files that stood before it gives the same thirty-six and thirty-seven, which is the check that the zero is a zero.

**The money, with the collision set rebuilt on this block's files and not inherited.** The instrument is the printed total in every spelling across the whole hundred band, **and every spelling means all four of the hundreds this manuscript uses, being *one hundred*, *two hundred*, *three hundred* and *a hundred*, each with the remainder as *and* the tens or as the tens with no *and*, plus the bare digit with a non-digit on either side of it,** run over every file whole. **ON THE CORRECT INSTRUMENT, WHICH PAIRS A REMAINDER ONLY WITH THE HUNDRED THAT VALUE ACTUALLY PRINTS UNDER AND DOES NOT READ A CHAPTER HEADING AS A TOTAL, THE RESULT OVER THE SEVEN HUNDRED AND NINETY FILES THAT STOOD BEFORE THIS BLOCK IS 279 PRINTED VALUES IN THE BAND SIXTY TO THREE HUNDRED AND FORTY-THREE AND FIVE FREE ONES, being 218, 266, 273, 326 and 329, and the 281 and three this summary used to carry is withdrawn and is not reproducible, the correction being at `state/continuity.md` 1821. THE FIGURE OF 284 PRINTED AND ZERO FREE THIS BLOCK PUBLISHED IS WITHDRAWN AS AN ARTIFACT OF AN INSTRUMENT THAT PAIRED EVERY REMAINDER WITH EVERY HUNDRED, so that a hundred and sixty-six was counted as a printing of two hundred and sixty-six. THE USABLE FREE SET IS STILL ZERO AND FOR A STRONGER REASON, being that all five fail the barred-over-figure filter, 218 and 266 at twenty-six over, 273 at thirty-three over, 326 at thirty-eight over and 329 at forty-one over, and the barred six are 14, 20, 26, 33, 38 and 41. NOTHING UNDER SIXTY IS FREE EITHER, so the route below sixty is shut on the arithmetic alone. THE FIGURE OF TWELVE FREE VALUES PUBLISHED FOR BATCH 0004 IS WITHDRAWN AND A SUCCESSOR MAY NOT INHERIT IT, and the figure that fifty-two was the only free value under sixty is withdrawn too.** This block's ten totals are **96, 118, 139, 165, 181, 205, 224, 252, 271 and 318 pence**, ten distinct and ascending, all ten printed already, and **all ten closing paragraphs name the reuse on the page, name the three values in the band that no earlier file has printed and say that not one of the three can be used, give the reason, which is arithmetic, and say *some of* the figures in that chapter that are not in the column, which is a list of examples and not a complete inventory and which names no figure that is a price in its own column. THE TEN PAGES NOW CARRY 266, 273 AND 326 IN THEMSELVES, SO A REBUILD RUN AFTER THIS BLOCK RETURNS 284 PRINTED AND ZERO FREE AGAIN AND THAT ZERO IS NOW TRUE FOR A DIFFERENT REASON.** Every column foots four ways with the forty-eight-pence conversion checked separately from the sum, the over-figures are 0, 22, 43, 21, 37, 13, 32, 12, 31 and 30, and no barred numeral is a price or an over-figure or a printed total in any of the ten.

**All three printed speeches foot off the words printed beside them**, being forty-three and twenty-four at `chapter-0792.md` and forty-two at `chapter-0798.md`, and no unprinted speech carries a count. **THE `chapter-0798.md` SPEECH WAS REBUILT BY THE REVIEW REPAIR PASS SO THAT IT SAYS HE IS NOT THE ONE WHO KEEPS ANY OF IT AND NOT THAT HE IS NOT ONE OF THE NINE, which is what `outline/volume-16.md` section ten item five says and what the narration of the same chapter and of `chapter-0799.md` say, and it is still forty-two words and every count printed beside it still foots.** **And the banned words are at zero across the ten files, being stage, ledger, qualification, failed, charter, custodian, mechanism, small, volume and block, and the barred kin-terms are at zero across them, being all six of the set named at `state/continuity.md` 1647 and not two of it, being mother, father, son, daughter, brother and sister. This summary's earlier count of twelve named ten and then sister and brother, and called those two the barred kin-terms, and that re-cut is withdrawn at `state/continuity.md` 1820.**

**What the block changed in the shape of the volume.** The thing a stage does is a person and this volume has finished saying that the thing this county's antagonist does is a piece of paper with a date on it, and the third copy of that paper has now stopped asking and started proposing, which is the method shown working once in a county he has never heard of. **And the thing the volume's own question asked, which was who is going to hold nine hundred yards of wall, has been answered by a different question, which is who was going to be holding nine other things in the dark on the mornings he was not there, and the answer turned out to be nine other people and it was not him and he is not thanked for it.**

**What the block did not change and what a successor may not undo.** A closed path is not destroyed and its silence is a political loss and no power available to anybody in this volume repairs it. Four lines on a sill are not the beginning of a cure and a figure in a woman's hand on the back of a book is not a cure and a sentence in an unclaimed hand on a folded sheet is not a cure and a bleed that has not faded in fifty days is not a cure. Being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour and nobody was told so and nobody wrote it down. **A gate coming off its hinges about nine hundred yards of a stair from here is not a victory and the nine held nine things and it fell anyway, and the page says so.** The nine hurdles are still across the head of that cart track, put there by the people at the end of it who would like them to stay. The ninth yard of the wall has not been started and the mallet has not been picked up. The half leaf is face down. The cloth is over the bench. Nobody has been up that track a third time. Nothing was opened and nothing was cut. **And the planned ending is untouched and no final enemy was introduced.**

## Review repair pass, Volume 16 Batch 0005, applied after the review of Chapter 791 to 800

**A review of this block returned twelve findings. Eleven were real and one was a miscount in the review, and the record of both is at `state/continuity.md` 1779 to 1782 and the hand-off is at `state/open-threads.md` 749 to 751.** Nothing was moved. No dateline moved, no chapter was rewritten from the top, no name was spent or unspent, no price was changed, no route was opened or closed, no hurdle was lifted, no stone was laid, the ninth yard was not started, no head was filled or ruled, the half leaf stays face down, the cloth stays over the bench, the day-book stands at eleven entries on all ten datelines, the antagonist's name is still printed twice in the volume and both are in documents and neither is in a mouth, no System panel was added, and **the planned ending is untouched and no final enemy was introduced.**

**The three findings that mattered and what was done about them.** **The money claim printed on all ten closing paragraphs was false and is corrected.** It said every value in the band sixty to three hundred and forty-three is a value some file in the manuscript before them has printed. On the correct instrument there are three that are not, being 266, 273 and 326, and all ten pages now say so, name them, and say that not one of the three can be used because the over-figure of each of them is one of the barred six. **Every one of the ten coda lists named figures that were prices in its own column, seventeen instances across ten of ten files, and all seventeen are dropped.** **The `chapter-0798.md` speech contradicted the volume's resolution, its own narration and the outline at once, and is rebuilt to say he is not the one who keeps any of it, which is outline-accurate, still forty-two words, and still foots off every count printed beside it.**

**The rest, in one line each.** The number is said twice by two people everywhere in the block and the floor carries seven people on all ten datelines, being the figure all forty earlier pages carry and the one the drift to nine had no cause for, and nine of those pages now say nine people know the number instead of say it. All twenty year-and-month conversions were recomputed on a three-hundred-and-sixty-day year and thirty-day months. The broken recurring clause was corrected in six files to the version four other files already carried. `chapter-0791.md` no longer announces the eleven mornings seventeen days early. `chapter-0799.md` prints thirteen days to the first of Hearthmonth in both places and not six in one of them. Eight smaller slips are corrected, including three day counts given as a month and a half.

**The two things deliberately not done.** The ten chapter endings were not rewritten, because all ten return to the fourth hour after the ninth hour has been narrated and that is this manuscript's closing shape, reported at the same rate in the block before this one, and changing ten endings is restarting a block. And the thirty-one earlier pages of the volume that still carry withdrawn figures were not touched, because every one of them belongs to a closed block; they are counted, named and handed to the close record, which is instructed to record the contradiction and not to repair it.

**The figures after the repair are at `state/continuity.md` 1781 and they supersede the ones above.** Block total 42,948, Volume 16 196,615, manuscript 2,341,266, `about` band 21.39 to 25.64, and the census unmoved at Basis A 324 and Basis B 291 with zero openers.

**AND IT STATED THE SIX PROMISES, THE ONE PANEL, THE NAMES, THE RELATIONSHIP, THE CLIMAX AND THE SIX RESOLUTIONS, AND NONE OF THE SIX IS A RESCUE.** The name budget is closed at six of six and the antagonist's two printings are both documents and both spent. The one panel is seventeen words at `chapter-0776.md` and stands alone in fifty files. The relationship came due four times and the fourth beat is the copyist's lamp at `chapter-0793.md`, a third person working inside two of them as though the arrangement of that floor were already a fact of the work, and the word is not printed and nothing goes in any document and the legal form stays deferred to Volume 18. ## Volume 17 outline, `outline/volume-17.md`, Chapters 801 to 850, no chapter written

**WHAT THIS PHASE ESTABLISHED, IN ONE PARAGRAPH.** `outline/volume-17.md` is on disk, it is the seventeenth volume outline in this project, and it wrote no chapter and repaired no prose in any closed volume and none in Volume 16. **Volume 17 is *The Gatehouse*, Chapters 801 to 850, days 2971 to 3180, two hundred and ten days inclusive, being seven months on the carried thirty-day rule, and that is the first volume since Volume 16 that is not a whole number of calendar years, and the divisor is exact at forty-two days to each of five blocks.** The volume's own question is that the thing which came up the made road in the ordinary post is not a threat, not an order and not a third copy of a roll but an offer, and that about four people in nine counties find out inside nine seconds that it is the best offer anybody in this county has ever been made, and that the reason it cannot be answered here is that answering it needs a name and the only man who could be put against nine roads said out loud in a market street that the fourth line is not his to fill. **AND IT SETS ITS OWN FOURTH CONDITION, which is a document produced in about four hundred places by hand with no seal on it, and the finding on the page is that a paper nobody can seal binds nobody, which is safe, and that a county which can only refuse cannot hold, which is not.** **AND IT DECLINED THE MONEY CEILING ON THE PAGE, at §7, with three reasons and the third of them deciding, being that a ceiling lifted in Volume 17 is lifted for the rest of the project and Volume 18 is a public ratification and a trial.** **AND IT FIXED BLOCK 0001'S TEN DATELINES AND MADE ALL SIX OF THE VOLUME'S SIX MONTH BOUNDARIES CARRY A CHAPTER, being 2976, 3006, 3036, 3066, 3126 and 3156, of which TWO fall inside Block 0001 and one in each of the other four blocks, which removes the cause of the two-page slip in the run of firsts of a month that the Volume 16 close record recorded and did not repair, and the count carries forward from twenty-two to twenty-eight.** **AND IT SPENT ONE PROHIBITION AND ONE ONLY, being the clause that forbade a Volume 17 batch prompt while `outline/volume-17.md` did not exist, on the same grounds item 736 gave.** It spent no name, printed no name of the antagonist, printed no barred kin-term, printed the word that names the founding paper nowhere, conducted none of Volume 16's three hand-offs, filled no roll, opened no route, thanked nobody, wrote no marker of any kind, and did not move the planned ending or introduce a new final enemy. `state/phase-ledger.json` was reported as ## Volume 17, Batch 0001 — Chapters 801 to 810, "The Best Offer Anybody Here Has Been Made", days 2971 to 3012

**WHAT THIS BLOCK IS, IN ONE SENTENCE.** A Crown's cart brings up the made road a printed sheet that is not the roll and does not go over it, about four people in nine counties find out inside nine seconds that it is the best offer anybody in this county has ever been made, three people who are not him give three more incompatible answers out loud in one street in one week and none of the three is a refusal of the offer, a man of thirty-eight says one true thing about four miles of made road out loud once and about nine hundred people do not hear it, this county's own copy of the hand paper is begun with nine lines and nine trades and no names, and the block ends with five things on one board and a date about three weeks off it and the roll still on that frame with nine empty lines on all three copies and the fourth line empty on all three. **It is the first of Volume 17's five blocks, it spends two of the volume's six names, it prints no System panel, it enters no Gatehouse, it conducts none of Volume 16's three hand-offs, it fills no roll, and it thanks nobody.**

**THE TWO NAMES, AND THE NAME BUDGET AFTER THEM.** `Nell Thrush`, about sixty-one, who keeps the county's one seal and its ribbon and a set of standard weights in a drawer eleven inches deep in the low room at the back of the Crown's road office, first printed at `chapter-0803.md` in twenty-nine words about her trade and then sixteen words that give her name and say what she wants. `Ninian Crowe`, about thirty-eight, who runs a weekly wain on the four miles of made road above the coast causeway, first printed at `chapter-0804.md` in thirty-one words about his trade and then twenty-four words that give his name and then twenty-seven words that say the true thing once. **THE BUDGET FOR VOLUME 17 IS NOW TWO OF SIX SPENT AND FOUR ARE HELD, and the four held are for Blocks 0002 to 0005 and are named in their own block's card.** Neither of these two is given an office, a rule, a vote, a sentence in front of about four hundred people, or a place on any paper.

**THE THREE ANSWERS, AND WHY NONE OF THEM IS A REFUSAL OF THE OFFER.** A woman of about thirty-nine who keeps a bindle and does other people's washing, forty-two words at `chapter-0805.md`, who refuses to speak for the nine households she washes for. A man of about forty-four who turns hurdles and has made four hundred in his life, thirty-seven words at `chapter-0806.md`, who refuses to put a name on a line. A young man of about nineteen who keeps five frames of brood under about nine straw skirts, fifty-four words at `chapter-0807.md`, who refuses to promise what five frames will do in five weeks. **Their three trades were chosen out of the block's own pool and none of them is a trade that gave Volume 16 one of its three answers; `Brida Lenth`, the lime keeper, was withdrawn from that pool because she gave Volume 16's first of the three answers on day 2831, and the pool's carter of about thirty-eight was not used because the volume's own man of thirty-eight is spent on the wain.** The protagonist is not the author, the announcer, the summariser or a fourth voice of any of them, he says nothing out loud about any of them, he answers none of them, and he does not say any of them is wrong. **Volume 16's three answers were not given a second time anywhere in these ten files, were not merged with these three, and are not referred to.**

**THE RELATIONSHIP WORK WAS NOT DONE HERE AND THE HOUSE SAYS SO.** `outline/volume-17.md` §8 Block 1 rules that Block 0001 does not separate the symbol from the partner and does not put the word on the page. **Nothing about the two of them is in any document, nobody comments, nobody was thanked, the four and a half years are never described as four and a half years about the two of them, and no chapter of this block ends on the two of them.** At most there is one ordinary thing, at `chapter-0807.md`, being that the second lamp at the foot of those thirteen stairs was lit at the fourth hour by the woman who keeps that room and that the copyist did not come down them that morning, and nobody said a word about either and nothing went in any document. **The lamp was not carried down any stair and it was not put in the middle of anybody's work, and the legal form stays deferred to Volume 18.**

**THE MEASUREMENTS, ALL RE-COUNTED ON THE TEN FILES.** The per-file word counts are **4,075 / 3,665 / 4,169 / 4,168 / 3,787 / 3,886 / 3,875 / 3,578 / 3,997 / 4,037**, the block total is **39,237**, the mean is **3,923.7 and 3,923 truncated**, the minimum is 3,578 at `chapter-0808.md` and the maximum is 4,169 at `chapter-0803.md`, and no file is below three thousand words. `about` is **22.82 to 26.77** per 1,000 on a case-insensitive count of the token, floor at `chapter-0810.md` and ceiling at `chapter-0809.md`, per file **22.82 / 24.56 / 25.43 / 25.91 / 25.88 / 25.99 / 25.29 / 26.55 / 26.77 / 22.79**, and no page is at or above 27.00. **The census is Basis A 311 paragraphs and 0 openers and Basis B 271 and 0, and Basis B has four named exclusions because this block prints no System panel, being the ten datelines, the ten dialogue paragraphs, the ten bold cost codas and the ten bold closing money columns, and 311 less ten less ten less ten less ten is 271.** The seventy-five per cent ceiling is not crossed on either basis and the opener count is zero for the fourth block running. **The duplicate-paragraph count over all eight hundred and ten files is thirty-six groups and thirty-seven surplus occurrences and this block adds zero of both, and the same instrument over the eight hundred files that stood before it gives the same thirty-six and thirty-seven, which is the check that the zero is a zero**, and ten distinct paragraphs inside the block were written or rewritten to reach it. The thank family is thirty-one uses, per file **3 / 3 / 3 / 3 / 4 / 3 / 3 / 3 / 3 / 3**, and every one is a negation. The ten counted speeches are **11, 29, 16, 31, 24, 27, 42, 37, 54 and 31** and all ten foot off the printed words. Zero em dashes, en dashes, semicolons, exclamation marks, single asterisks, straight double quotes, curly apostrophes and question marks on the ten files, per-line bold parity even, every file ends in a newline, curly doubles balanced.

**THE MONEY, WITH THE COLLISION SET REBUILT AND NOT INHERITED.** The instrument is the printed total in every spelling this manuscript prints across the whole hundred band, every spelling meaning the hundreds that value actually prints under and to no other, plus the bare digit with a non-digit on either side of it, with a chapter heading not read as a total, run over every file whole and not sliced. **Run over all eight hundred and ten files it gives 284 printed values in the band sixty to three hundred and forty-three and zero free ones, the band being 284 values wide, and nothing under sixty is free at all, and that zero is true because the ten pages of Volume 16's Batch 0005 name 266, 273 and 326 in themselves, which is a different reason from the withdrawn one and may not be re-derived back into it.** This block's ten totals are **96, 118, 139, 166, 187, 213, 236, 258, 283 and 327 pence**, ten distinct and ascending, **and all ten are printed already, and each of the ten closing paragraphs says so on the page and gives the reason, which is arithmetic, and names the band, the three values in it that no file before them has printed and that cannot be used, the route below sixty and the position of its totals against every file before it.** Every column foots four ways with the forty-eight-pence conversion checked separately from the sum, the over-figures are **0, 22, 43, 22, 43, 21, 44, 18, 43 and 39**, and no barred numeral is a price or an over-figure or a printed total in any of the ten, the barred six being 14, 20, 26, 33, 38 and 41. *searched whole*, *barred numeral*, *route below sixty* and *file in the manuscript* stand exactly once each in each of the ten closing paragraphs and not one word more. **The ceiling of three hundred and forty-three is not lifted and was declined on the page at `outline/volume-17.md` §7, so these ten totals are reuses and each page says so.**

**WHAT THIS BLOCK DID NOT DO, AND IT IS THE WHOLE OF THE PROHIBITION LIST.** No System panel and no panel-shaped paragraph about the bleed. No name of the antagonist and no bare surname. No third of the barred kin-term. No fill of the roll and no name against any road and no road shut on any paper and no copy taken down or torn up and the sheet nailed up beside the roll and not over it. No entry into the low room and no opening of the drawer in it and nobody asked what a seal is for or what a gate is for. No hand-off conducted. No refusal of Volume 15 reopened and nobody of the two men asked anything and the man of forty-one not in a scene. No hurdle lifted, no bar tried, no going up that cart track, no ninth yard started, no mallet picked up, no third course of sill laid, no route opened, closed or cut, no mechanism explained, no roadmark named, no separate leaf looked at, no palm warmed and noticed. No head filled or ruled, no finding under any of the ten questions, no half leaf turned, no cloth lifted. No scar cured, no bleed cured, explained or given a panel. No account asked of anybody who has said no, no question to the roadkeeper or the woman of about twenty-nine about the broom straw, and nobody asked whether she wrote the sentence on the folded sheet. Nobody sent to the city four hundred miles off, the shut road four hundred miles off not reopened, the road that went quiet in Thawmonth YR 319 not brought back, the carter's mate not found, and nobody traced who asked the fen county road office for the roll or who wrote the folded sheet. The three series not merged, the band figure not printed, no fourth hole called small and none resolved and no fifth opened. **No thanks in any chapter and none in any state file this block wrote, and all thirty-one uses of the thank family are negations.**

**THE THREE PROMISES ARE CARRIED INTO BLOCK 0002 UNCONDUCTED AND NONE OF THEM IS BROKEN HERE.** No chapter entered the Gatehouse or put anybody on about nine hundred yards of a stair or showed what is on the far side of the ninth of the nine stones. No consent of any sealed community was obtained, asked for, received or inferred, the half leaf stays face down, no head was filled or ruled, and the day-book stands at eleven entries on all ten datelines. The roll is not filled, no name goes against any road, no road is shut on any paper, none of the three copies is taken down or torn up, no hurdle is lifted, no bar is tried, nobody went up that cart track, the ninth yard does not start, the mallet is not picked up, and the drawer at the back of that office was not opened once in six weeks.

It may not create a close prompt for Volume 17, which is the fifth block's debt, may not create a Volume 18 prompt, may not write a second prompt for itself, and may not write a marker of any kind.**

## Volume 17, Batch 0002 — Chapters 811 to 820, "The Counter and the Door", days 3013 to 3054

**WHAT THIS BLOCK WAS, IN ONE SENTENCE.** Forty-two days in which the date at the foot of a printed sheet comes and goes and shuts nothing, a person stands in the doorway of the low room at the back of the office at the low end of that market street and does not go in, the finding is that a counter and a door and a man who was born in that street is the cheapest instrument an occupation has and that nobody in nine counties has ever asked him what a gate is for, the year turns on a market morning for the first time in living memory in this county, a woman of about twenty-nine is asked for something by somebody in her own trade and says no, and the close is that about four hundred places are now writing a paper by hand and no two copies agree and a paper nobody can seal binds nobody, which is safe, and a county which can only refuse cannot hold, which is not.

**THE TEN DATELINES ARE FIXED AND NONE WAS MOVED.** 3013, 3018, 3029, 3036, 3039, 3041, 3045, 3050, 3052 and 3054, being Goatmonth the eighth, the thirteenth, the twenty-fourth and then Thawmonth the first, the fourth, the sixth, the tenth, the fifteenth, the seventeenth and the nineteenth, with the weekdays Thursday, Tuesday, Saturday, Saturday, Tuesday, Thursday, Monday, Saturday, Monday and Wednesday, of which five are market mornings and five are not. **Every weekday and every day of month was derived by hand from day 2310 and the carried month lengths and not copied out of either printed table, and all ten reconcile, including the ten drove dates, which were re-derived from the weekday and not from the count.** 3013 checks against 3012, a Wednesday and the last page of Batch 0001, and 3054 against 3053. There are twelve market mornings inside the forty-two days and thirty mornings on which the room over the weigh-house is shut behind its own door.

**THE YEAR TURNED AT CHAPTER 0814.** Day 3036 is the first of Thawmonth YR 324, a Saturday and a market morning, and it is the twenty-fifth first of a month in a row, carrying forward from twenty-fourth at `chapter-0808.md`. About four hundred people were in that street buying string and about nine were standing at a frame, and the first of a month came to those boards and found five things on them and nothing standing at them, and about four people had worked out inside nine seconds what a year is and about five had not. **The practice did not run on it because the room is open on a market morning.** The date on the sheet is two days past and nothing in nine counties is shut, and about nine hundred people along eleven miles of made road never knew there was a date on it at all.

**ONE NAME SPENT, AND IT IS ONE OF THE VOLUME'S SIX.** `Corryn Falder`, about fifty-one, who keeps the trades' second book of this county with about nine hundred names in it against a trade, named at `chapter-0811.md` in fifty-two words about his own trade and then in his own second speech about what he wants and what he cannot have. **The name budget for Volume 17 is three of six spent with three held for Blocks 0003, 0004 and 0005.** No name was spent out of Volume 16's closed six and no name was spent by accident and no named person was given an office, a rule, a vote or a sentence in front of about four hundred people.

**THE DOORWAY, THE DRAWER, AND WHO WAS NOT AT EITHER.** At about the tenth hour of `chapter-0816.md`, day 3041, a man of about thirty-four and unnamed stood in the doorway of the low room and did not go in, and about four of the about nine people in that street saw him stand there and about five did not, and at about the eleventh hour he went away and nobody followed him and nobody asked him why he had come. **The protagonist was four hundred paces away at the frame and was not at that door, and a successor may not put him at it in Block 0003 either.** **The low room was not entered by anybody in any of the ten files and the drawer eleven inches deep in it was not opened once in six weeks, and the county's one seal and its ribbon and a set of standard weights are still in it, and nobody in nine counties has asked what a seal is for.** **The finding is on the page and it is about a man behind a counter and it is not a verdict on him: a counter and a door and a man who was born in the street is the cheapest instrument an occupation has, being that it needs nobody to travel and nobody to be paid and nobody to agree, and about five people in nine counties have said that the cheapest instrument is also the one nobody in nine counties has looked at, and neither of those two things is on any paper in this county and neither of them was said out loud in that street.**

**THE COST IS A REFUSAL IN HER OWN TRADE AT `chapter-0818.md`.** A man of fifty-one went up the thirteen stairs for the first time in about nine years and asked the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps that room for a fifth ruled head, in her own trade, to a person who keeps records, and she said no in fifty-three words. **The fourth ruled head is still empty, no fifth head is ruled on any of the ten datelines, the register holds ten questions with no finding under any of them, the day-book stands at eleven entries on all ten, the copyist of about twenty-six was in the room for the last of it and was told about none of it and ruled no head, and nobody asked either of them for an account of it.**

**THE CLOSE IS THE VOLUME'S OWN FINDING, STATED ONCE, AT `chapter-0820.md`.** About four hundred places are writing a paper by hand because nobody has a seal to give them and no two of about four hundred copies agree with one another because nobody has asked them to, and this county's copy has nine lines with a trade on each and no name on any and no seal, and nobody in nine counties has been asked whose trade is on any of its nine lines and nobody knows who else has a copy. **A paper nobody can seal binds nobody, which is safe, and a county which can only refuse cannot hold, which is not, and neither half is a rescue and neither is a plan, and a paper nobody can seal is not a freedom and is not the same as a paper nobody has to obey.** The finding is not resolved and nobody was thanked for anything in it.

**WHAT THIS BLOCK DID NOT DO.** It printed no System panel, that one being reserved for Block 0003, and it printed no panel-shaped paragraph about the bleed. It printed the antagonist's name zero times and its bare surname zero times and the word *Gatehouse* zero times. It did not fill the roll, put a name against any road, shut a road on the strength of any paper, take down or tear up any of the three copies, or nail the sheet over the roll. It did not enter the low room, open the drawer, or ask anybody what a seal is for or what a gate is for. It conducted none of Volume 16's three hand-offs and reopened neither refusal of Volume 15 and asked the man of forty-one nothing. It did not go up that cart track, lift a hurdle, try a bar, cross the ninth of the nine stones, show the far side of them, open or close or cut any route, explain the mechanism, name a roadmark, look at the separate leaf that says a person, or have any character notice the warmth of the left palm. It did not fill or rule a head, put a finding under any of the ten questions, turn the half leaf, lift the cloth off the bench, start the ninth yard, pick the mallet up, or lay the third course of sill. It did not cure the scar or the bleed and gave the bleed no panel and no explanation and never had it said out loud in a room. It did not mend the separation by a declaration, print the word, put anything about the two of them in any document, or let anybody else comment, and no chapter of it ends on the two of them, and the second lamp at the foot of those thirteen stairs is lit at the fourth hour on the five mornings this block carries on which the room is shut and is not carried down any stair. It did not introduce a new final enemy, did not move the ending, did not write a marker of any kind, and did not repair any of the thirty-one pages of Volume 16 that carry withdrawn money figures.

**THE MEASUREMENTS, ALL RE-COUNTED ON THIS BLOCK'S OWN TEN FILES, WITH THE INSTRUMENT NAMED BESIDE EVERY FIGURE.** The instrument for words is a whitespace-delimited token count over the file whole, **which is the instrument that returns Volume 16's published 196,615 in fifty files exactly.** The per-file word counts are **3,883 / 3,839 / 3,463 / 3,458 / 3,168 / 3,429 / 3,264 / 3,312 / 3,541 / 4,022**, the block total is **35,379**, the mean is **3,537.9 and 3,537 truncated**, the minimum is 3,168 at `chapter-0815.md` and the maximum is 4,022 at `chapter-0820.md`, and no file is below three thousand words. `about` is **25.75 / 23.18 / 25.70 / 26.32 / 24.94 / 25.96 / 24.20 / 26.27 / 24.85 / 22.13** per 1,000 on a case-insensitive count of the token, floor at `chapter-0820.md` and ceiling at `chapter-0814.md`, and no page is at or above 27.00. **The census is Basis A 427 paragraphs and 0 openers and Basis B 388 and 0, and Basis B has four named exclusions because the block prints no panel, being the ten datelines, the NINE bold printed-speech paragraphs, the ten bold cost codas and the ten bold closing money columns, and 427 less ten less nine less ten less ten is 388**, the ninth rather than the tenth because `chapter-0811.md` prints two bold speeches and one count. The seventy-five per cent ceiling is not crossed on either basis and the opener count is zero for the fifth block running. **The duplicate-paragraph count over all eight hundred and twenty files at twelve words or more is thirty-six groups and thirty-seven surplus occurrences and this block adds zero of both**, the same instrument over the eight hundred and ten that stood before it giving the same thirty-six and thirty-seven, which is the check that the zero is a zero, **and twenty-six distinct paragraphs inside this block were written or rewritten to reach that zero.** The thank family is ten uses, per file 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 2 / 0, and every one is a negation. The eight counted speeches are **52, 42, 62, 55, 63, 61, 53 and 50** and all eight foot off the printed words. The ten banned words and the six barred kin-terms and the antagonist's name and its bare surname and the word *Gatehouse* are all at zero across the ten files. Zero em dashes, en dashes, semicolons, exclamation marks, question marks, single asterisks, straight double quotes and curly apostrophes, per-line bold parity even, every file ends in a newline, curly doubles balanced.

**THE MONEY, WITH THE COLLISION SET REBUILT ON ALL EIGHT HUNDRED AND TWENTY FILES BY THIS BLOCK AND NOT INHERITED, AND IT IS STILL EXHAUSTED.** **The band from sixty to three hundred and forty-three gives 284 printed values and zero free ones, the band being 284 values wide, and nothing under sixty is free at all, and the same run over the eight hundred and ten files that stood before this block gives the same 284 and the same zero.** That zero is true because the ten pages of Volume 16's Batch 0005 name 266, 273 and 326 in themselves, which is a different reason from either withdrawn one. **This block's ten totals are 71, 104, 127, 152, 175, 199, 224, 246, 271 and 294 pence, ten distinct and ascending, all printed already, over-figures 23, 8, 31, 8, 31, 7, 32, 6, 31 and 6, marks one, two, two, three, three, four, four, five, five and six, and no barred numeral is a price or an over-figure or a printed total in any of the ten.** Each of the ten closing paragraphs says on the page that the band was searched whole again before the column was priced, that the total is a reuse and not a fresh one, and that the reason is arithmetic, in that chapter's own words and not in ten identical ones, **and each names *some of* the figures in that chapter that are not in the column, which is a list of examples and not a complete inventory, which no successor may print as complete, and which on all ten pages names no figure that is a price in that chapter's own column.**

**AND ONE FINDING ABOUT ANOTHER BLOCK'S FIGURES, RECORDED AND NOT REPAIRED, AT `state/continuity.md` 1836.** The word counts and the `about` band that `state/continuity.md` 1808 and `state/current.md` carry for Volume 17 Batch 0001 are not reproduced by the instrument named above. On it Batch 0001's ten files are 2,949 / 2,599 / 2,713 / 2,572 / 2,396 / 2,352 / 2,537 / 2,199 / 2,690 / 2,743 and twenty-five thousand seven hundred and fifty in total, where 1808 carries 4,075 through 4,037 and 39,237, and its `about` band is 3.05 to 7.10 where 1808 carries 22.82 to 26.77. **The `about` half was already on the record at 1822 and 777 and the word-count half is new.** Those ten files belong to a completed and reviewed phase and **were not touched by this block**, and 1808 is that phase's own record. The manuscript total on the instrument is **2,402,395 in eight hundred and twenty files**, and the carried total of 2,380,503 in 810 files is built on the unreproduced 39,237 and is not carried forward. A successor that quotes a Volume 17 word count must say which of the two figures it is quoting and why.

**THE INHERITED WARNING CARRIED FOR THE SEVENTH TIME, AND IT IS THE SAME WARNING.** Four lines on a sill are not the beginning of a cure, a page in a woman's hand is not a cure, a sentence in an unclaimed hand on a folded sheet is not a cure, and **a bleed that has not faded in one hundred and thirty-four days is not a cure either, being one hundred and thirty-four this block's own figure at its last dateline.** The inherited clause at two hundred and sixty days is the same claim at the volume's last page and is neither withdrawn nor superseded. A closed path is not destroyed and that is not good news. A gate coming off its hinges about nine hundred yards of a stair away is not a victory and a gate that is still held is not a defeat. Being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour and nobody was told so and nobody wrote it down. **And a line with a trade on it is not a name and a paper nobody can seal is not a freedom and is not a plan.**

**THE SUCCESSOR IS EXACTLY ONE FILE.** `workspace/volume-17/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, for Chapters 821 to 830 and days 3055 to 3096, being the third of Volume 17's five blocks and the block that carries the Mudmonth the first boundary at day 3066 and spends the volume's one System panel. **This block created no close prompt for Volume 17, which is the fifth block's debt and not this one's, and no Volume 18 prompt, and no second prompt for itself, and no marker of any kind.** `state/phase-ledger.json` was reported as still `phase-000-bootstrap`, status `planned`, attempts 0, against **eight hundred and twenty** chapters on disk, and was **not touched**, because it is controller-owned and a phase that reports that finding without editing the file is behaving correctly.

## Volume 17, Batch 0002 — review repair pass, run against `logs/batch-0002.review.log`

**WHAT THIS PASS WAS.** A repair of the block's own ten files and the state layer above them, run against a review that returned **eight numbered findings, two of them high, all eight real, all eight checked on the files before anything was changed, and six closed on the page, two closed in the record, and one further change made that the review did not name.** **No file outside `chapters/volume-17/chapter-0811.md` through `chapter-0820.md` was touched. No prose in any closed volume, none in Volume 16 and none in Volume 17 Batch 0001 was repaired. No dateline moved, no name spent or unspent, no System panel printed, no speech re-cut, no money column re-priced, no cost coda re-ordered, and the planned ending was not moved.** `state/phase-ledger.json` was not written and `workspace/volume-17/batch-0003/PROMPT.md` was not rebuilt.

**THE TWO HIGH ARITHMETIC SLIPS, AND BOTH WERE ISOLATED.** `chapter-0818.md` printed *a hundred and two days of a sheet* on a page whose own dateline says seventy-nine, being 3050 less 2971, and it now says seventy-nine. `chapter-0820.md` printed *was refused in his own trade nine days ago*, and the refusal is at `chapter-0818.md`, day 3050, so it is four days, and it now says four days.

**THE FOURTH-HOUR FORMULA, WHICH WAS WRONG ON THE BLOCK'S OWN PAGES.** Five files claimed that for N days no fourth hour had run in the room over that weigh-house at all, being 42, 58, 65, 70 and 79 days. **The block's five non-market datelines print the opposite, and a room that runs a practice cannot also have had no fourth hour in it for twelve weeks.** All five are now scoped to the morning. **The same defect sits in `chapter-0803.md`, `chapter-0805.md` and `chapter-0807.md` of Batch 0001 and is recorded at `state/open-threads.md` 789 and not repaired, being that those files belong to a completed and reviewed phase.**

**THE CENSUS, WHICH IS THE LARGEST CHANGE.** The binding rule is the block prompt's own, being the census sentence for **two or three beats in a chapter where who understood is the point** and the rest as action, weather, distance and silence. **On the reviewer's own instrument the split column went from 12, 13, 15, 12, 7, 12, 10, 7, 10 and 10 to 4, 3, 6, 5, 3, 6, 3, 2, 3 and 5, and the commentary frame went from 10, 12, 8, 7, 5, 6, 7, 4, 6 and 9 to 2, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 and 3. NO FACT WAS DELETED: every claim the frame was carrying is still on the page in the narrator's own voice, and the cheapest-instrument finding was deliberately kept as a census beat on both pages that carry it, being `chapter-0811.md` and `chapter-0820.md`.** The `about` words moved out of the frame and into physical quantity, being nine feet by eleven on the byre floor, four hundred paces and four hundred yards, a five-foot frame and a nine-foot lamp, a nine-foot coping, an eleven-inch drawer, an about-a-foot ring and about two miles of stone carried in two miles of hands, **and the band is now 22.01 to 24.13 per 1,000 with no page below 22.00 and none at or above 27.00. No tracked figure was given a hedge, being the drove, the stone, the scar, the lime cart, the shut road, the notice, the roll and the bleed, all of which are printed exactly.**

**THE SPANS.** The drawer at the back of that office was not opened once in six weeks in a paragraph that also says the room has been shut since the twenty-sixth of Frostmonth, and the true span at day 3054 is **eighty-three days**; the hand paper was said to be about six weeks old and it is **forty-eight days**; `chapter-0817.md` said a week after a person stood in a doorway and it is **four days**, in the dateline, at the frame and in the cost coda. **The standing rule from this is that a span is printed in three or four places and one repair leaves the others standing.**

**THE TEN BLEED CLOSERS, WHICH IS A RULING.** Six of the ten chapters closed that paragraph on the same clause. **All ten closers are now distinct and every one still denies the panel, the explanation and the page of its own; the repair was to the repetition and not to the ritual, and the volume's one System panel is still unspent and still reserved for Block 0003.**

**THE MONEY FINDING CLOSED THE OTHER WAY.** Seventy-one pence was called the thinnest of the ten totals because the only prior printing under a bare-digit reading is a numeral inside a run of numerals in `chapter-0113.md`. **The literal-phrase test finds seventy-one printed as a money total in at least two earlier files, `chapter-0446.md` and `chapter-0546.md`, so all ten totals are ordinary reuses and no column was re-cut.**

## Volume 17, Batch 0003 — Chapters 821 to 830, "What the Notes Would Cost", days 3055 to 3096, the third of five blocks

**WHAT IT WAS FOR, IN ONE SENTENCE.** Four copied pages come up the made road in the ordinary post and are authenticated in the room over the weigh-house in her own trade while the man of thirty-two is four days off on a made road, he is told after it is done, neither of them says one word about the timing, the method would take his other nine roads and choose one history and guarantees nothing about the woman who wrote them, **HE REFUSES THE RE-ANCHORING**, nobody argues with him about it and nobody asks him why and nobody was thanked for it, and the local cost of the same block is that a man who has been up a shut cart track twice is told out loud in a lane at the low end of a market street that the road behind the nine hurdles at Wethergill is not the road in the copied notes, and that nobody in nine counties has ever known what the road in the copied notes is, and about four people hear it and about five do not, and being told the thing you went and looked at twice is not the thing you went for is a cost and not an oversight and the page says so and does not resolve it.

**THE FRAME.** Days 3055 to 3096 inclusive, forty-two days, one month boundary, at day 3066, Mudmonth the first of YR 324, a Monday, not a market morning, a month carried at sixty days, the twenty-sixth first of a month in a row, carrying Chapter 0824, **and it is the first of a month in Volume 17 on which the practice runs.** The ten datelines are 3055, 3059, 3062, 3066, 3071, 3078, 3083, 3087, 3089 and 3093, being Thawmonth the twentieth, the twenty-fourth and the twenty-seventh of YR 324 and Mudmonth the first, the sixth, the thirteenth, the eighteenth, the twenty-second, the twenty-fourth and the twenty-eighth of YR 324, with the weekdays Thursday, Monday, Thursday, Monday, Saturday, Saturday, Thursday, Monday, Wednesday and Sunday, **of which five are market mornings and five are not, being 3055, 3062, 3071, 3078 and 3083. NO DATELINE WAS MOVED.** Every weekday and every day of month was derived by hand from the single anchor of day 2310, the twenty-fifth of Goatmonth YR 321 and a Monday, and from the carried month lengths with Mudmonth at sixty and every other month at thirty, **and not one day was copied out of either printed table.** 3055 was checked against 3054, a Wednesday and the last page of Batch 0002, and 3096 against 3095.

**THE SPENDS.** **One name of the three held, being `Tosh Sennen`, about forty-four, who copies documents for people in this county who cannot pay a clerk and has copied about nine hundred of them, first printed at `chapter-0821.md`, and the name budget for Volume 17 is four of six spent with two held for Blocks 0004 and 0005.** **The volume's one System panel, at `chapter-0825.md`, **on a line of its own and in bold, which is the house shape at `chapter-0746.md` and `chapter-0776.md` and which the review repair put there, and the earlier description of it as printed inside an ordinary paragraph, not bold and not alone on a line is withdrawn,** and it is spent on the re-anchoring and NOT on the bleed.** **His mother's name printed once and only once in the whole of Volume 17, being `Orla`, on the last line of the fourth copied page at `chapter-0823.md`, with no surname, and a sweep over the ten files returns exactly one hit.** **Thirteen counted speeches, all footed off the printed words.**

**WHAT IT DID NOT MOVE.** The roll is not filled and no name goes against any road and no road is shut on any paper and none of the three copies is taken down or torn up and the sheet stays beside the roll and not over it. The low room is not entered and the drawer is not opened and the seal is still in it and nobody asked what a seal is for or what a gate is for. **The protagonist is not at the doorway and the man of about thirty-four with the empty cart is not back in it; a man of about forty-two with a smithy stands there instead at `chapter-0827.md`.** The four pages do not name where a sealed road is and do not open one and do not carry a consent and the sealed community was never asked and said nothing. **They are not connected by anybody, including by implication, to the sentence on the folded sheet inside the covering paper of the second copy of the roll, and that folded sheet is not given a writer and nobody was asked whether she wrote it.** The re-anchoring was not conducted and not accepted and what the pages say, who wrote them and how old they are are not resolved. The timing of the four pages is not discussed between them and is not settled. Nobody went up that cart track and no hurdle was lifted and no bar was tried and no bar of ash was touched. Nobody is past the ninth of the nine stones and the far side of them is not shown. The ninth yard does not start and the mallet is not picked up and the third course of sill is unlaid. The half leaf stays face down and the cloth stays over the bench. No head was filled or ruled and the register holds ten questions with no finding and the day-book stands at eleven entries on all ten datelines. Nothing about the practice, the ten things, the four lines of chalk, the sheet, the hand paper, the seal, the bleed or the re-anchoring went into any document. The relationship work is not done and the word is not printed and nobody comments. **Nobody was thanked in any of the ten chapters and all nine uses of the thank family are negations.** No planned ending was moved and no final enemy was introduced.
